# Katman Agaci - Hedef Topoloji + 5440 Tip Kaydi

**Zorunlu Baglantilar:** [mimari index](../architecture/index.md) · [catalog](../architecture/catalog/index.md) · [layer-rules](../.rules/layer-rules.md) · [ADR-030](../.decisions/accepted/ADR-030-5000-layers-class-level.md) · [layer-5000](../architecture/layer-5000/index.md)

> **Tek cumlelik sorumluluk:** *hedef mimari nasil bir agac ve agacta toplam kac katman (tip kaydi) var* sorusunun tek cevabi; her yaprak `N<NN>-<NNN>` ID ile katalogdaki satirlarin birebir kendisidir.

## §0 Hedef Topoloji (olcum: 2026-10-02)

| Agac | Klasor | .csproj | Cekirdek | Infrastructure.* | Semsiye |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `versacoder.old.1\src` | 36 | **36** | 7 (UI ile) | **29** | yok |
| `versacoder.old.2\src` | 36 | **34** | 8 (Infrastructure + UI) | **28** (Reporting YOK) | VAR |
| `versacoder.old.old old\src` | 36 | **36** | 7 (UI ile) | **29** | yok |
| **bugun** `C:\versacoder` | 9+2 | **11** | 9 (Desktop+Cli) | **0** | VAR |
| **HEDEF** | - | **40** | 9 | **29** + 2 test | K1`de silinir (G7) |

**Union 29 paket (3 agac birlestirimi):** AI · Auth · Backup · Caching · CodeAnalysis · Config · Context · Data · Deployment · Diagram · Documentation · FileSystem · Git · Integration · Learning · Logging · MCP · Messaging · Network · Observability · Plugins · ProjectAnalysis · Refactoring · Reporting · Security · Services · Templating · Testing · Versioning

**Topoloji formulu:** `9 + N + 2` = 9 + 0·5·12·29 + 2 test = **11 · 16 · 23 · 40**

---
